# Commands · J · part 1

1,421 entries, j-add-git-hook to journal-finder. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **j-add-git-hook**: Add git pre-commit hook to the current project ([jruz/agent-config](https://github.com/jruz/agent-config/blob/HEAD/commands/j-add-git-hook.md))
- **j-arch**: Architecture consultation — API design, distributed patterns, and system design. Use when designing APIs, choosing architecture patterns, or making technology decisions. ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-arch.md) · ★2)
- **j-audit**: Security threat model and vulnerability scan — STRIDE analysis, SAST patterns, and compliance mapping. Use when reviewing code for vulnerabilities, conducting threat modeling, or mapping compliance controls. Do NOT use for quick security questions (ask directly instead). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-audit.md) · ★2)
- **j-brainstorm**: Design-before-implementation workflow — explore intent, requirements, and design before writing code. Use when creating features, building components, or modifying behavior. Do NOT use after implementation is started (use /j-execute-plan instead). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-brainstorm.md) · ★2)
- **j-browse**: INTERACTIVE browsing - user guides, the agent clicks. Browse and interact with any web page via ChromeDevTools MCP, token-efficient (text snapshots; a screenshot only when text is not enough, read by an agent). Use when user says browse, naviga, open page, apri questa pagina, click on, fill the form, or to reproduce a bug in the UI. To run the project's end-to-end checklist use /dev:j-e2e instead. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-browse.md))
- **j-bug**: Fix a bug (junior workflow). Goes through the bug path of development-workflow.md — LISTEN, DIAGNOSE with Protocol D, PLAN — on its own branch and task folder, then STOPS; the user triggers /dev:j-cvm-exec-plan to execute the fix. Use when the user reports a bug, something is broken, non funziona, nefunguje, or needs to fix an error. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-bug.md))
- **j-checks**: Audit project linting setup against checks skill, reconcile differences ([jruz/agent-config](https://github.com/jruz/agent-config/blob/HEAD/commands/j-checks.md))
- **j-ci-setup**: Set up a project's CI/CD (junior workflow). A production branch; a pull request from the main branch to it runs the final gate; merged, it deploys. On GitHub or GitLab, read from the remote; the merge automatic or by hand, as the user decides. Optional, one project at a time. Use when the user says CI, CD, CI/CD, pipeline, branch production, or /dev:j-setup asks and the answer is yes. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-ci-setup.md))
- **j-close**: Close a feature or a bug and deploy (junior workflow). The CLOSE phase of development-workflow.md — verifies completion, reads the change against the mindsets, runs the final gate, merges as @merge-strategy says, optionally deploys, rotates the Memory Bank. Use when work is done, user says close, merge, chiudi, hotovo, or feature is ready for production. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-close.md))
- **j-cloud**: Cloud architecture consultation. Use when designing cloud infrastructure, optimizing costs, or planning deployments. Do NOT use for container/Kubernetes questions (use /j-devops instead). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-cloud.md) · ★2)
- **j-code-review**: Review code using project skills (justfile, rust, sqlx) ([jruz/agent-config](https://github.com/jruz/agent-config/blob/HEAD/commands/j-code-review.md))
- **j-commit**: Create atomic commits following the commit skill, ensuring each commit passes just check ([jruz/agent-config](https://github.com/jruz/agent-config/blob/HEAD/commands/j-commit.md))
- **j-config-audit**: Security audit of Claude, Codex, Gemini, and shared agent configuration for secrets, over-broad permissions, and prompt-injection vectors. Use when reviewing agent config after changing settings, hooks, or permissions. Do NOT use for application code security (use /j-audit). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-config-audit.md) · ★2)
- **j-continue**: Continue existing work (junior workflow). Resumes a feature or a bug from where it stopped — reads the Memory Bank's state, checks it against the branch and the task folder, re-reads what the phase needs, picks up the thread. Use when the user says continue, riprendi, pokracuj, or starts a new session on existing work. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-continue.md))
- **j-create-pr**: Automate PR workflow — stage, commit, branch, push, and open a GitHub PR. Use when ready to submit changes for review. Do NOT use if changes are incomplete (finish implementation first). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-create-pr.md) · ★2)
- **j-cvm-check-plan**: Validate a plan — TDDAB or Step — with the CVM parser. Shows the plan's type, its blocks and the validation errors with their lines. Use when the user says check plan, validate plan, controlla piano CVM. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-cvm-check-plan.md))
- **j-cvm-exec-plan**: Parse and execute a plan — TDDAB or Step — with the CVM planexecutor, block by block, in the phases the planexecutor runs. Resumes after an interruption. Use when the user says execute plan, esegui piano, run plan CVM. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-cvm-exec-plan.md))
- **j-data**: Data engineering consultation. Use when building data pipelines, optimizing queries, or designing data platforms. Do NOT use for basic SQL questions (search references/data/ for quick help). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-data.md) · ★2)
- **j-debug**: Systematic bug investigation — root cause analysis, not random fixes. Use when a test fails, a bug appears, or behavior is unexpected. Do NOT use for asking how to fix something (code directly instead). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-debug.md) · ★2)
- **j-debug**: Debug a problem while looking for it, with Protocol D, explaining each step (junior workflow). It finds and explains the cause and changes no production code — a bug is fixed with /dev:j-bug. Full verbose mindset — use when context window is not a concern. For large context sessions use /dev:j-debug-mb instead. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-debug.md))
- **j-debug-mb**: Debug any problem with Protocol D - MBEL compact version (junior workflow). Uses ~70% fewer tokens than /dev:j-debug — prefer this in large context sessions. Same methodology, compressed mindset. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-debug-mb.md))
- **j-defrag**: Scan the codebase for fragmentation — duplicated components, inconsistent patterns, files to consolidate, logic to share — and fix it in verified batches. Use to keep the codebase coherent on a regular cadence. Do NOT use for single-file cleanups (edit directly) or diff review before merge (use /j-diff-review). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-defrag.md) · ★2)
- **j-demo**: Demo command. Use when demonstrating the command format. ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/evals/fixtures/kb-mini/.claude/commands/j-demo.md) · ★2)
- **j-deploy**: Deploy to production (junior workflow). The DEPLOY phase of development-workflow.md — runs the final gate, the backup, the deploy script, and verifies. Use when the user says deploy, metti in produzione, nasad, or push to production. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-deploy.md))
- **j-devops**: DevOps consultation — CI/CD, containers, IaC, and observability. Use when configuring Docker, Kubernetes, Terraform, GitHub Actions, or monitoring. Do NOT use for cloud provider selection (use /j-cloud instead). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-devops.md) · ★2)
- **j-diff-review**: Deep multi-perspective diff review - code quality, security, testing gaps, documentation drift, observability gaps, and language-specific gotchas. Fixes what it finds, files large unrelated items in the repo's tracker, and opens or updates the PR. Use when reviewing a diff or PR before merge. Do NOT use for simple code questions (ask directly instead). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-diff-review.md) · ★2)
- **j-doc-sync**: Post-ship documentation sync — discover and fix stale docs after shipping changes. Use when docs may be out of date after a release or feature merge. Do NOT use for writing new docs from scratch (use /j-docs instead). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-doc-sync.md) · ★2)
- **j-docs**: Documentation consultation — technical writing, API docs, and changelogs. Use when writing docs, generating OpenAPI specs, or automating changelogs. Do NOT use for inline code comments (write directly). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-docs.md) · ★2)
- **j-e2e**: Run the manual E2E checklist in the browser with ChromeDevTools (junior workflow). Executes the test cases of tests/e2e-manual/template.md against the running app — on any web project, with or without automated end-to-end tests. Use when the user says run e2e, testa il frontend, spusti e2e testy, or wants to verify UI behavior end-to-end. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-e2e.md))
- **j-e2e-generate**: Generate the manual E2E checklist from the use cases (junior workflow). Writes tests/e2e-manual/template.md — the scenarios /dev:j-e2e runs by hand in the browser through ChromeDevTools — from the tasks' requirements and the Memory Bank's productContext. Works on any web project, with or without automated end-to-end tests. Use before /dev:j-e2e, or when user says generate tests, crea test, vygeneruj testy. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-e2e-generate.md))
- **j-email-analysis**: Process AI research newsletter emails (TLDR AI, The Batch, Import AI, etc.) -- extract links, categorize, download paper PDFs, and produce a structured summary. Use when processing research newsletters. Do NOT use for non-research emails (handle manually instead). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-email-analysis.md) · ★2)
- **j-email-consolidate**: Consolidate a directory of per-email AI newsletter analysis reports into a single unified weekly digest. Use when merging emails/*.md per-email reports. Do NOT use for processing a single email (use /j-email-analysis). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-email-consolidate.md) · ★2)
- **j-execute-plan**: Execute a written implementation plan task-by-task with verification gates — inline batches or a fresh subagent per task, picked from the plan; each PR boundary closes with /j-diff-review, which opens the PR. Use when you have a saved plan ready to implement. Do NOT use for creating the plan (use /j-plan) or ad-hoc changes without a plan. ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-execute-plan.md) · ★2)
- **j-explain**: Generate a self-contained HTML document or information report that explains a topic — inline CSS/JS/SVG, visual hierarchy, spatial diagrams, no external dependencies. Use when you want a shareable, scannable explainer or findings report instead of a wall of markdown. Do NOT use for quick answers or inline comments (respond directly) or markdown project docs (use /j-docs). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-explain.md) · ★2)
- **j-frontend**: Frontend consultation — frameworks, design systems, and web patterns. Use when building with React, Next.js, Svelte, Tailwind, or solving frontend challenges. Do NOT use for backend API design (use /j-arch instead). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-frontend.md) · ★2)
- **j-help**: Help (junior workflow). Shows all available junior commands with descriptions. Use when the user asks what commands exist, how junior workflow works, or says help/aiuto/pomoc. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-help.md))
- **j-info**: Show JavaScript runtime and package manager versions ([827652549/mako-study-monorepo](https://github.com/827652549/mako-study-monorepo/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-info.md))
- **j-init**: Scaffold a repository for Claude, Codex, and Gemini -- agent config, project settings, toolchain, and supporting docs, derived from what the repo already has. Use when starting a new project, when a repo has no agent config, or when its config is stale or partial. Do NOT use for creating knowledge-base assets such as commands or skills (use /j-new). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-init.md) · ★2)
- **j-init-secrets**: Initialize a project's secret management with git-crypt (a .00-secrets/ folder transparently encrypted in git). Run in the EARLY phase of a project. Installs git-crypt, configures .gitattributes/.gitignore, generates the key and tells the user to save it in a password manager, writes a runbook + security guide. SAFE/idempotent: if git-crypt is already initialized it does NOT re-init (reuses the key); if a secrets dir already has content it stops and destroys nothing. Use when "init secrets", "setup git-crypt", "secret management", new project. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-init-secrets.md))
- **j-lang**: Language patterns consultation — idioms, tooling, and project scaffolding. Use when setting up a project, choosing patterns, or working in Python, JS/TS, Go, Rust, Swift, or other languages. Do NOT use for framework-specific help (use /j-frontend or domain specialist instead). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-lang.md) · ★2)
- **j-mcp-setup**: Install and configure MCP servers for the project. Universal — works with both Claude Code (.mcp.json / mcpServers) and opencode (opencode.jsonc / mcp). Interactive — asks which MCPs to add, auto-installs everything, merges into the right config. Use when user says mcp setup, add mcp, configura mcp, or wants to enable AI tooling. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-mcp-setup.md))
- **j-new**: Scaffold a new .claude/ command, skill, or agent with correct structure and best practices. Use when creating any new .claude/ asset. Do NOT use for editing existing assets (edit directly). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-new.md) · ★2)
- **j-new-feature**: Start a new feature (junior workflow). Creates the feature branch and task folder and goes through the phases of development-workflow.md up to PLAN — what the user needs, the code and the mindsets that apply, what already exists, the structure — writes the plan, reviews it, then STOPS. The user triggers /dev:j-cvm-exec-plan to execute it. Use when the user wants to add something new, implement a feature, aggiungere funzionalita, or pridat novu feature. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-new-feature.md))
- **j-new-project**: Start a project with the junior workflow (junior workflow). A project to create goes through the phases of development-workflow.md up to PLAN — what the project is, the mindsets that apply, what already exists, the structure — has /dev:j-setup record the profile and wire the lint gate, writes the TDDAB plan of the skeleton and the Memory Bank, then STOPS; the user triggers /dev:j-cvm-exec-plan. A project that already has code is wired, described and given its Memory Bank, without a plan. Use when starting a brand new project from scratch, or bringing an existing one into the workflow — nuovo… ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-new-project.md))
- **j-next**: Advance a multi-PR plan to its next part - confirm the last part landed in origin/main, branch fresh off main, execute the next phases, and open the PR. Use after an earlier PR from the plan was merged. Do NOT use for a plan's first PR (use /j-execute-plan) or to refresh an already-open PR (use /j-rebase). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-next.md) · ★2)
- **j-observe**: Observability consultation -- logging, metrics, tracing, alerting, and analytics instrumentation. Use when designing observability systems, adding instrumentation, setting up dashboards, or defining SLOs. Do NOT use for CI/CD pipeline config (use /j-devops) or cloud infrastructure (use /j-cloud). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-observe.md) · ★2)
- **j-paper**: LaTeX research paper consultation -- templates, section editing, compile + log analysis, source research, citation integration, and submission readiness. Use when scaffolding a new paper, updating sections, debugging build errors, integrating cited sources, or preparing a venue submission. Do NOT use for general LaTeX syntax questions (read references/research/latex-paper-writing.md directly) or non-academic documents (use /j-docs). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-paper.md) · ★2)
- **j-paper-analysis**: Analyze an ML/AI research paper: structured summary, critical evaluation, and reviewer-style assessment using the three-pass method. Use when reviewing an academic paper, arxiv preprint, or conference submission. Do NOT use for quick paper summaries (ask directly instead). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-paper-analysis.md) · ★2)
- **j-plan**: Research-orchestrated implementation planning - parallel specialist lenses, then a phase-skeletoned TDD plan with exact paths, verification commands, and PR boundaries. Use when you have requirements and need a plan before coding. Do NOT use for executing an existing plan (use /j-execute-plan) or exploring what to build (use /j-brainstorm). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-plan.md) · ★2)
- **j-plan**: Create PLAN.md to break down work into atomic, committable steps ([jruz/agent-config](https://github.com/jruz/agent-config/blob/HEAD/commands/j-plan.md))
- **j-plan-status**: Report progress on PLAN.md vs actual branch changes ([jruz/agent-config](https://github.com/jruz/agent-config/blob/HEAD/commands/j-plan-status.md))
- **j-poc**: Create proof of concept for unknown/complex features (junior workflow). Explores feasibility in isolated docs folder, no production code. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-poc.md))
- **j-pr**: Prepare and push PR automatically, then report what was created. ([jruz/agent-config](https://github.com/jruz/agent-config/blob/HEAD/commands/j-pr.md))
- **j-pr-fix**: Resolve all PR reviewer comments — categorize, fix, reply inline, verify, and push. Use when you have open PR review comments to address. Do NOT use if there are no comments yet (wait for review). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-pr-fix.md) · ★2)
- **j-rebase**: Squash a branch to one commit, rebase onto latest origin/main, verify the conflict resolutions with targeted tests, and force-push to the open PR. Use when an open PR needs to be refreshed against main. Do NOT use on main/master, or before a PR exists (use /j-create-pr). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-rebase.md) · ★2)
- **j-rebase**: Stash changes, rebase on main, and pop stash ([jruz/agent-config](https://github.com/jruz/agent-config/blob/HEAD/commands/j-rebase.md))
- **j-release**: Release what is on the main branch (junior workflow). Opens the pull request from the main branch to the production branch; its CI runs the final gate, and once merged — by itself or by hand, as /dev:j-ci-setup decided — the CD deploys. Use when the user says release, rilascia, manda in produzione, nasad, on a project with CI/CD. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-release.md))

- **j-review-claudemd**: Analyze recent conversation history to improve CLAUDE.md files — find violated instructions, missing patterns, and outdated rules. - **j-review-plan**: Review a plan — TDDAB or Step — against its methodology, the project's mindsets and the real code, before the user sees it and before it runs (junior workflow). Use when the user says review plan, controlla il piano, skontroluj plan, or before executing a plan. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-review-plan.md))
- **j-save**: Save and leave (junior workflow). Commits WIP, updates Memory Bank, pushes to remote. Use when the user says save, pause, stop, leave, I'm done for now, or wants to preserve progress before ending the session. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-save.md))
- **j-setup**: Setup/validate Junior workflow settings for this project. On an existing project it wires ai-agent in — detects language, stack, commands, ports, the mindsets profile and the lint gate, and writes j-settings.md. On a project not created yet it writes the base j-settings.md and leaves the rest to /dev:j-new-project. MUST run first before any other j-* command. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-setup.md))
- **j-skill-audit**: Audit the .claude/ knowledge base - skills, commands, agents, references, config, cross-references, documentation currency, and auto-memory hygiene for conformance and integrity. Use when creating or modifying any .claude/ asset to validate compliance. Do NOT use for quick checks (inspect files directly instead). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-skill-audit.md) · ★2)
- **j-stack-dev**: Run JStack enterprise workflow in single-lead mode ([JarodFroneman/jstack](https://github.com/JarodFroneman/jstack/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/j-stack-dev.md) · ★1)
- **j-status**: Show current status (junior workflow). Shows branch, state, progress, and pending work. Use when the user asks where are we, what's the status, cosa manca, co sme robili, or wants a summary of current work. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-status.md))
- **j-step**: A feature built in small steps the user drives (junior workflow). Each run is one step — the user says one piece, the piece goes through the phases of a feature at its own size, ONE TDDAB block is written, approved and executed with the planexecutor, then it STOPS and the user checks. The first run opens the branch and the task folder; the next ones add a step to the same feature; /dev:j-close closes it. Use when the user wants to build step by step, a piece at a time, un pezzo alla volta, passo passo, po kroku, or does not know the whole feature yet. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/j-step.md))
- **j-system-design**: Comprehensive system design suite — produce architecture documents covering multiple perspectives. Use when designing a new system or documenting existing architecture. Do NOT use for quick design questions (use /j-arch instead). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-system-design.md) · ★2)
- **j-tdd**: Autonomous TDD implementation loop — write tests first, run to confirm failure, implement until green, lint, commit. Use when adding a feature or fixing a bug with test-driven development. Do NOT use for debugging existing failures (use /j-debug). ([jlaws/dotfiles](https://github.com/jlaws/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/j-tdd.md) · ★2)
- **ja-compare**: Head-to-head comparison of two or more journals. Provide journal names and (optionally) your paper attributes to get a dimension-by-dimension verdict table. ([Zaious/journal-atlas](https://github.com/Zaious/journal-atlas/blob/HEAD/.claude-plugin/commands/ja-compare.md) · ★2)
- **ja-contribute**: Contribute your publishing experience to the Journal Atlas knowledge base. Share your submission/rejection/review experience with a journal and I'll convert it into a structured Markdown entry or patch for PR submission. ([Zaious/journal-atlas](https://github.com/Zaious/journal-atlas/blob/HEAD/.claude-plugin/commands/ja-contribute.md) · ★2)

- **jd-score**: JD评分：18条规则审计+5维10分制人工打分，输出可发布的量化评分表与改进清单。 ([lhg-plugs/lhg-job-desc](https://github.com/lhg-plugs/lhg-job-desc/blob/HEAD/commands/jd-score.md))
- **jd-versions**: JD多版本：一份岗位信息输出招聘网站版（完整六章+搜索关键词）/内推版（300字内+钩子）/猎头版（画像+卖点）。 ([lhg-plugs/lhg-job-desc](https://github.com/lhg-plugs/lhg-job-desc/blob/HEAD/commands/jd-versions.md))
- **jd-work-plans**: Browse and create John Deere work plans ([CoreyFransen08/john-deere-ops-mcp](https://github.com/CoreyFransen08/john-deere-ops-mcp/blob/HEAD/commands/jd-work-plans.md) · ★10)
- **jdex-audit**: Audit a Johnny.Decimal system by comparing the JDex against the folder structure ([ngerakines/jd](https://github.com/ngerakines/jd/blob/HEAD/jd/commands/jdex-audit.md) · ★7)
- **jdi-add-phase**: Registers a new phase in ROADMAP.md. Slug-as-ID — multi-developer safe. Validates slug uniqueness and shape. Append at end (default), or position via --before/--after. Atomic commit. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/runtimes/claude/commands/jdi-add-phase.md) · ★4 · 6 copies)
- **jdi-adopt**: Entry point for brownfield project (code already exists). Runs jdi-adopter — scan + analysis + confirmation + generates .jdi/ with adopted=true flag. Replaces /jdi-new for existing projects. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/runtimes/claude/commands/jdi-adopt.md) · ★4 · 6 copies)
- **jdi-bootstrap**: Creates per-project doer + reviewer specialists. Runs after /jdi-new, before /jdi-discuss. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/runtimes/claude/commands/jdi-bootstrap.md) · ★4 · 6 copies)
- **jdi-confirm-dod**: Interactive loop to confirm DoD manual items in the phase REVIEW.md. Required when verify returned APPROVED_PENDING_MANUAL. Each item asks user keep/confirm with evidence or remain pending. Accepts slug or position. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/core/commands/jdi-confirm-dod.md) · ★4 · 6 copies)
- **jdi-create**: Creates new JDI agent or skill via validated question loop and automatic integration. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/core/commands/jdi-create.md) · ★4 · 6 copies)
- **jdi-discuss**: Adaptive question loop to capture locked decisions before planning the phase. Accepts slug or position. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/runtimes/claude/commands/jdi-discuss.md) · ★4 · 6 copies)
- **jdi-do**: Executes phase. Automatic routing to project's doer specialist. Wave-based parallel if a wave has &gt;=2 independent tasks. Accepts slug or position. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/runtimes/opencode/commands/jdi-do.md) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **jdi-do**: Executes phase. Automatic routing to project's doer specialist. Wave-based parallel if phase has &gt;=3 independent tasks. Accepts slug or position. ([slipalison/bezel](https://github.com/slipalison/bezel/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jdi-do.md) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **jdi-handoff**: Hand this session off to an unattended agent-jdi run (quits it, then resumes in the background) ([tanghong123/claude-replay](https://github.com/tanghong123/claude-replay/blob/HEAD/integrations/claude/commands/jdi-handoff.md) · ★3)
- **jdi-issue**: Fully autonomous intake — turn a task/issue/card (GitHub, Linear, Jira, Azure DevOps, Trello, or pasted text) into shipped work with a pull request at the end, no human in the loop. Compensates the missing human with harder gates — DoD critic forced on, warnings get a fix round, hard iteration fuses. Humans return only to review the PR. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/core/commands/jdi-issue.md) · ★4 · 5 copies)
- **jdi-loop**: Ralph loop — orchestrates auto dev↔review until APPROVED verdict. 5 iter cap, human gate + reset (max 3 resets = 15 iter absolute). Oscillation detection cuts dead loop early. Accepts slug or position. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/runtimes/claude/commands/jdi-loop.md) · ★4 · 6 copies)
- **jdi-migrate-phases**: Migrates a v1 project (numeric NN-slug phase folders) to v2 (slug-as-ID schema). Non-destructive — does NOT rename existing folders. Stamps schema_version: 2 + current_phase_slug in STATE.md. Idempotent. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/runtimes/claude/commands/jdi-migrate-phases.md) · ★4 · 5 copies)
- **jdi-migrate-phases**: Migrates a v1 project (numeric NN-slug phase folders) to v2 (slug-as-ID schema). Non-destructive — does NOT rename existing folders. Adds schema_version + phases.json manifest. Idempotent. ([slipalison/simulator-ccb](https://github.com/slipalison/simulator-ccb/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jdi-migrate-phases.md))
- **jdi-new**: Entry point for new project. Runs research + asker, generates PROJECT.md + ROADMAP.md. --auto (alias --yolo) skips all questions — the researcher decides everything itself, using web/context7 research when available, and records the rationale. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/core/commands/jdi-new.md) · ★4 · 5 copies)
- **jdi-new**: Entry point for new project. Runs research + asker, generates PROJECT.md + ROADMAP.md. ([slipalison/simulator-ccb](https://github.com/slipalison/simulator-ccb/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jdi-new.md))
- **jdi-next**: The one command to remember. Derives where the current phase is from its artifacts and runs the correct next step — discuss, plan, do, verify, confirm-dod, ship, or fix after a BLOCKED review. Zero arguments needed. --loop (or orchestration.next_execution "loop" in config.json) makes the execute/verify states run the bounded ralph loop instead of single steps. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/core/commands/jdi-next.md) · ★4 · 5 copies)
- **jdi-plan**: Generates phase PLAN.md. Decomposes into tasks with files_modified, acceptance, parallelism waves. Accepts slug or position. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/runtimes/claude/commands/jdi-plan.md) · ★4 · 6 copies)
- **jdi-remove-phase**: Removes a phase from ROADMAP.md. Accepts slug OR position. Refuses to remove done/current/past phases. Archives any existing phase artifacts. Atomic commit. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/runtimes/claude/commands/jdi-remove-phase.md) · ★4 · 6 copies)
- **jdi-ship**: Finalizes phase after verify. Writes the SHIPPED.md marker in the phase folder, advances STATE hint to next phase. ROADMAP.md untouched (conflict-free for teams). --pr opens a pull request via gh (best-effort). Accepts slug or position. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/runtimes/claude/commands/jdi-ship.md) · ★4 · 5 copies)
- **jdi-ship**: Finalizes phase after verify. Updates ROADMAP.md, marks phase as done, advances pointer to next. Accepts slug or position. ([slipalison/simulator-ccb](https://github.com/slipalison/simulator-ccb/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jdi-ship.md))
- **jdi-status**: Prints a compact summary of where the project is — current phase, what the last action did, and the exact next command to run. --stats adds outcome metrics derived from artifacts (first-pass rate, loop iterations, lead time). Read-only. No agent invoked. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/runtimes/claude/commands/jdi-status.md) · ★4 · 5 copies)
- **jdi-status**: Prints a compact summary of where the project is — current phase, what the last action did, and the exact next command to run. Read-only. No agent invoked. ([slipalison/simulator-ccb](https://github.com/slipalison/simulator-ccb/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jdi-status.md))
- **jdi-verify**: Runs phase quality gates via reviewer specialist. Build, tests, coverage, lint, security checks, UI validation, Definition of Done. Verdict APPROVED / APPROVED_WITH_WARNINGS / APPROVED_PENDING_MANUAL / BLOCKED. Accepts slug or position. ([slipalison/jdi-cli](https://github.com/slipalison/jdi-cli/blob/HEAD/runtimes/claude/commands/jdi-verify.md) · ★4 · 6 copies)
- **jds-to-oneui-figma**: Redesign legacy JDS / Testlab Figma screens into OneUI components ([prasadbhandari27/Jiosense](https://github.com/prasadbhandari27/Jiosense/blob/HEAD/oneui/oneui-mcp/assets/commands/jds-to-oneui-figma.md) · 2 copies)
- **JD匹配**: 用法: - `/JD匹配 inputs/简历.docx` — 然后粘贴 JD 内容 - `/JD匹配 inputs/简历.docx inputs/JD.txt` — 直接传两个文件 - `/JD匹配 [直接粘贴 JD 链接或内容]` — 用 profile.json 里登记的简历 ([shangsitongshizaitiantang/industry-resume-toolkit](https://github.com/shangsitongshizaitiantang/industry-resume-toolkit/blob/HEAD/commands/JD%E5%8C%B9%E9%85%8D.md) · ★533)
- **jean:agent-creator**: Claude Code 에이전트(.claude/agents/&lt;name&gt;.md)를 설계·작성·검증한다. Skill과 달리 에이전트는 오케스트레이터가 위임하는 격리된 전문가. 모델 선택·도구 권한·입출력 계약 설계에 집중. eval 루프로 품질 검증. "에이전트 만들어줘", "서브에이전트 설계해줘", "전문가 위임 구조 만들어줘", "agent 추가해줘", "코드 리뷰 전담 에이전트", "병렬 처리용 에이전트", "격리 컨텍스트 필요해" 등 Claude Code 에이전트 파일이 필요한 상황에서 먼저 호출할 것. ([JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills](https://github.com/JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jean%3Aagent-creator.md))
- **jean:analyze**: 프로젝트 코드베이스 전체를 자동 스캔해 6개 영역(아키텍처·기능·워크플로우·데이터·건강도·온보딩) 리포트를 `docs/analysis/` 에 생성한다. 스택 자동 감지 (Python·Node·Go·Rust·Java/Kotlin·Ruby) + 프레임워크별 패턴 추출. 코드베이스 파악, 신입 온보딩 문서, 아키텍처 감사, 코드 건강도 점검, "이 프로젝트 어떻게 돌아가" 류 질문에 호출. 키워드 — 분석 리포트, 프로젝트 개요, 아키텍처 감사, 온보딩 가이드, 코드베이스 파악, project overview, architecture audit, code health, onboarding doc. ([JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills](https://github.com/JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jean%3Aanalyze.md))
- **jean:claude-md-creator**: 프로젝트를 분석해 Claude Code 최적화 CLAUDE.md를 생성하거나 기존 파일을 개선한다. "CLAUDE.md 만들어줘", "클로드에게 프로젝트 컨텍스트 주입해줘", "컨텍스트 파일 만들어줘", "새 프로젝트 셋업해줘", "프로젝트 설명 파일 만들어줘", "Claude가 프로젝트를 알게 해줘" 등 모든 CLAUDE.md 생성·갱신 요청에 사용. 팀 컨벤션 정의, 신규 프로젝트 셋업, 기존 파일 개선 모두 커버. 새 프로젝트 시작 시 가장 먼저 사용할 것. ([JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills](https://github.com/JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jean%3Aclaude-md-creator.md))
- **jean:deep-analyze**: 코드베이스 심층 분석. 사용자가 지정한 주제·이슈에 대해 코드 전수 조사 후 파일:라인 단위 상세 리포트 생성. 검증 루프(최대 3회)로 false positive 제거. "어디서 발생하는지", "찾아줘", "누락된 곳", "감사", "이슈 추적" 같은 자유 주제 질문에 호출. 주제 고정 없음 — N+1 쿼리, 중복 코드, 에러 핸들링, dead code, 보안 취약점, 성능 핫스팟 등 무엇이든. 키워드 — 심층 분석, 코드 감사, 이슈 추적, 어디서, 찾아줘, deep analysis, code audit, hotspot, vulnerability scan. ([JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills](https://github.com/JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jean%3Adeep-analyze.md))
- **jean:hook-creator**: 기존 스킬을 분석해 hook(`settings.json` 또는 `SKILL.md` frontmatter)으로 옮기면 좋은 결정적 동작을 찾아 제안·생성·검증·정리한다. 사용자가 직접 호출. 기존 hook과 중복·모순 자동 검출. dry-run 검증과 단계적 설치(local-first)로 안전성 확보. 스킬에서 추출하는 도구이므로 분석 대상 스킬이 먼저 존재해야 함. hook을 처음부터 만들고 싶을 때는 `.claude/settings.json`을 직접 편집할 것. ([JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills](https://github.com/JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jean%3Ahook-creator.md))
- **jean:idea**: 두서없는 생각·아이디어를 대화로 함께 다듬어 LLM이 효율적으로 실행할 수 있는 구조화된 명령으로 변환. 주제 무관 — 기술 구현·설계 고민·리서치·비즈니스 기획·문제 해결 모두 지원. "뭔가 좀 모호한데", "어떻게 시작할지", "구체화해줘", "정리해줘", "brainstorm" 같은 막연한 입력에 호출. 키워드 — 구체화, 정리, brainstorm, 아이디어 다듬기, 기획, 막연, 어디서부터. ([JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills](https://github.com/JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jean%3Aidea.md))
- **jean:rule-creator**: 기존 스킬을 분석해 rule(`CLAUDE.md` 또는 `.claude/rules/*.md`)로 옮기면 좋은 부분을 찾아 제안·생성·정리한다. 사용자가 직접 호출. 기존 rule과 중복·모순 자동 검출. 스킬에서 추출하는 도구이므로 분석 대상 스킬이 먼저 존재해야 함. rule을 처음부터 만들고 싶을 때는 `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` 또는 `.claude/rules/&lt;topic&gt;.md`를 직접 편집할 것. ([JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills](https://github.com/JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jean%3Arule-creator.md))
- **jean:skill**: 사용자 요구사항을 받아 jean:skill-creator / jean:rule-creator / jean:hook-creator / jean:claude-md-creator / jean:agent-creator 중 필요한 것을 선택·순서대로 실행하는 통합 오케스트레이터. 새 기능·자동화·행동을 처음부터 만들거나 기존 스킬을 올바른 프리미티브(스킬/룰/훅/CLAUDE.md/에이전트)로 리팩터링할 때 사용. 스킬 생성 시 eval pass_rate ≥ 0.95 달성까지 자동 루프. 모든 수정 시 사이드 이펙트 항상 검사. "만들어줘", "추가해줘", "이 스킬 정리해줘", "자동화해줘", "skill/rule/hook 만들어", "CLAUDE.md 만들어줘", "프로젝트 컨텍스트 설정해줘", "에이전트 만들어줘", "서브에이전트 설계해줘", "전문가 위임 구조", "어떤 프리미티브에 넣어야 해" 등 Claude Code 행동 정의가 필요한 모든 상황에서 가장 먼저 사용할 것. ([JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills](https://github.com/JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jean%3Askill.md))
- **jean:skill-creator**: 새 Claude 스킬을 만들거나 기존 스킬을 개선·평가한다. Anthropic 공식 prompting best practices (XML 태그·긍정 프레이밍·few-shot 3원칙·골든룰)를 스킬 작성 가이드에 통합. 사용자가 스킬을 처음부터 만들거나, 기존 스킬을 다듬거나, eval 로 성능을 측정하거나, description triggering 정확도를 높이고 싶을 때 호출. ([JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills](https://github.com/JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jean%3Askill-creator.md))
- **jean:skill-sync**: skills/agents/rules 파일 수정·추가 후 일관성 자동 점검·정리 스킬. 포맷 준수, 스킬 간 상호작용 계약, rules paths 불일치, 잔제 패턴, 유령 참조, 중복 내용을 탐지하고 수정한다. "/jean:skill-sync" 단독 실행 또는 jean:skill 완료 후 자동 호출. 인자로 파일명을 넘기면 해당 파일 관련 범위만 스캔. ([JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills](https://github.com/JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jean%3Askill-sync.md))
- **jean:tick-tock**: 사용자와 나눈 느슨한 수정 논의(대화, 코멘트, 생각 덩어리)를 어떤 AI 실행자(스킬·에이전트·일반 Claude Code)에도 바로 붙여넣을 수 있는 명확한 수정 브리핑으로 압축한다. 수정 지시가 모호하거나 흩어져 있을 때, AI에게 정확히 뭘 어떻게 고치라고 전달할지 막막할 때 호출. 독립 스킬 — jean 생태계 오케스트레이터와 연동하지 않음. ([JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills](https://github.com/JISU-JEONG/claude-custom-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/jean%3Atick-tock.md))

- **join.cs**: Jak spojit úsečky do křivky v AutoCADu příkazem SPOJ (JOIN): úsečky na jedné přímce v jednu, oblouky v kružnici, oblouky a spline ve spline. Vyzkoušejte online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/join.cs.md))
- **join.de**: So arbeitet AutoCADs VERBINDEN (JOIN, Alias VB): Linien und Bögen, die sich treffen, zur Polylinie, fluchtende Linien zu einer, Bögen zum Kreis. Gratis testen. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/join.de.md))
- **join.es**: Cómo funciona UNIR (JOIN, alias U) de AutoCAD en español: líneas y arcos que se tocan en una polilínea, líneas alineadas en una, arcos en un círculo. Pruébalo. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/join.es.md))
- **join.fr**: Comment fonctionne JOINDRE (JOIN, alias J) d'AutoCAD en français : lignes et arcs en polyligne, lignes alignées en une seule, arcs en cercle. Essayez-le. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/join.fr.md))
- **join.hu**: Az AutoCAD EGYBEFŰZ (JOIN) parancsa: csatlakozó vonalak és ívek vonallánccá, egy egyenesbe eső vonalak eggyé, ívek körré, görbék spline-ná. Próbálja ki ingyen. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/join.hu.md))
- **join.it**: Come funziona UNISCI (JOIN, alias U) di AutoCAD in italiano: linee e archi che si toccano in una polilinea, linee collineari in una, archi in un cerchio. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/join.it.md))
- **join.ja**: AutoCAD の JOIN[結合](J)の使い方を解説します。接する線分と円弧を 1 つのポリラインに、同一直線上の線分を 1 本に、円弧を円に結合します。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/join.ja.md))
- **join.ko**: AutoCAD 결합(JOIN, J) 사용법: 끝이 만나는 선과 호를 폴리선 하나로, 같은 직선 위의 선을 하나로, 호를 원으로, 곡선을 스플라인으로 합칩니다. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/join.ko.md))
- **join.pl**: Jak połączyć linie w polilinię w AutoCADzie poleceniem POŁĄCZ (JOIN): linie na jednej prostej w jedną, łuki w okrąg, krzywe w splajn. Wypróbuj za darmo online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/join.pl.md))
- **join.pt-BR**: Como funciona o JOIN do AutoCAD em português, UNIR (alias UNE): linhas e arcos que se tocam numa polilinha, linhas alinhadas numa só, arcos num círculo. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/join.pt-BR.md))
- **join.ru**: Как соединить отрезки в полилинию в AutoCAD командой СОЕДИНИТЬ (JOIN): отрезки на одной прямой — в один, дуги — в круг, кривые — в сплайн. Попробуйте онлайн. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/join.ru.md))
- **join.zh-CN**: CAD 怎么把线合并成多段线：AutoCAD 的 JOIN（快捷键 J）将首尾相连的直线和圆弧合并为多段线，共线的直线合并为一条，圆弧合并为圆。在浏览器中免费试用。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/join.zh-CN.md))
